# This Week in Marketing: Canva vs. Adobe, OpenAI's AGI problem, Q3 earnings & TikTok in the middle

![rw-book-cover](https://substackcdn.com/icons/substack/apple-touch-icon-1024x1024.png)

## Metadata
- Author: [[Jaskaran from The Social Juice]]
- Full Title: This Week in Marketing: Canva vs. Adobe, OpenAI's AGI problem, Q3 earnings & TikTok in the middle
- Category: #articles
- Summary: Big tech and AI drove huge Q3 gains and bold moves, with Amazon, Google, Nvidia and Microsoft posting strong results while investing heavily in AI. Canva and Adobe battled over creative tools and pricing as AI features spread across design and media. TikTok, streaming services, and ad markets face political, regulatory, and competitive pressure as companies trim costs and chase new AI-driven revenue.

##### What was that? Amazon's ad business grows, Google Search's first market share rebound, TikTok deal is stuck, Canva disses Adobe with new product launch, OpenAI's AGI problem and the rest is...

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/6e937f2e-6613-43f8-80fd-0ba402577997?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. 

> Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/969213ad-c1eb-4577-8007-d7c9499a94f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Canva takes [direct aim](https://substack.com/redirect/d07fa673-aecf-46c3-bd92-2e0fc6d7bc1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at Adobe’s egregious prices and moves [Affinity](https://substack.com/redirect/2e771546-dcb4-4d48-ad07-42b85e0f0f27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to a free model.
* Trump-Xi meeting proves [less than pivotal](https://substack.com/redirect/260b3c9a-f992-4dd6-b3de-f8b7ed09356a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for TikTok deal.
	+ Neither President Trump nor Chinese officials indicated any new developments for the popular video app.
* Amazon ad revenue [soars 24%](https://substack.com/redirect/5b2891ea-0b77-4ae1-b0c1-31170fb0de3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to $17.7 billion, net revenue increased 13% to [$180 billion](https://substack.com/redirect/fd2352eb-b09d-466e-b12f-36399e48226b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Q3, AWS revenue jumps 20%.
	+ Amazon confirms plans to lay off [14,000 corporate workers](https://substack.com/redirect/9a327866-c250-46b7-8c39-e0eaa1d997d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as part of wave of cuts.
* OpenAI gives [Microsoft 27% stake](https://substack.com/redirect/abaad365-a107-42d7-aeec-86a9657a99eb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), completes [for-profit](https://substack.com/redirect/82ef577f-b4cb-4cdb-96c7-ea82a2936de1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shift.
	+ OpenAI has an [AGI problem](https://substack.com/redirect/36368774-fb3b-4ecd-ab40-ac3aa2f36fc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) — and Microsoft just made it worse.
* [Netflix](https://substack.com/redirect/5cf9981c-efd3-4615-bdda-fc71fac415f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is actively [exploring](https://substack.com/redirect/dd9979fc-57d1-4988-ad5b-6591aef02211?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [a bid](https://substack.com/redirect/dd9979fc-57d1-4988-ad5b-6591aef02211?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Warner Bros. Discovery Inc.’s studio and streaming businesses.
* Meta brings in [record quarterly revenue](https://substack.com/redirect/25021085-6ed3-4f98-b975-ba37297bc04c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Q3 sales rise 26% YoY, but [a major tax bill](https://substack.com/redirect/716734b4-3aa7-4314-aea9-76a1c0eb65fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) dampens earnings per share.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!zM4F!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/a93aff30-fb67-4100-bd89-8ab85e214f27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Google ads hit [$74.18 bn](https://substack.com/redirect/6e8a0e94-7a2c-44f9-806a-fac4444522c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Alphabet crosses [$100 bn barrier](https://substack.com/redirect/080be7d6-b413-4164-8fc0-b41e62cb09be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Q3 2025.
* Pinterest launches [new AI Assistant](https://substack.com/redirect/a031de02-a997-4b29-ae14-1c768c1de09d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to enhance online shopping experience & product discovery.
* [Adobe Max 2025](https://substack.com/redirect/f2225478-222b-421d-bad5-660262c8e714?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): all the [latest creative tools](https://substack.com/redirect/aef9abea-f845-4c2c-8c67-5a6f7e97efd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and [AI announcements](https://substack.com/redirect/62a51662-693c-4fd4-a918-3dcbfb4b9738?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Adobe [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/8161fdbd-2aef-4b0c-b0f0-65147c2abb30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI assistants for Express and Photoshop.
	+ Adobe now lets you [generate](https://substack.com/redirect/c6541fe2-6b71-42bc-b412-575475cfc398?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) soundtracks and speech in Firefly.
* Reddit’s sales [jumped 68% YoY to $585 million](https://substack.com/redirect/f9729e8f-d087-4120-a19d-0896ff25a330?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the third quarter, Daily Active Uniques increased 19% YoY to 116.0 million.
* U.S. Senators announce bill that would [ban AI chatbot companions](https://substack.com/redirect/05f3cf93-b735-489a-a27d-988762205006?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for minors.
	+ Character.AI bans [users under 18](https://substack.com/redirect/42924f80-31d0-4714-a620-7a18c3fe2dac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after being sued over child’s suicide.
* Roblox shares drop after saying plans to [prioritize safety impact](https://substack.com/redirect/4bf47bc5-e0dc-42c2-a071-e5b24615fa34?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) may weigh on growth next year.
* Judge rules for [publishers](https://substack.com/redirect/3dda27e4-5afa-434b-9f84-cb287a1037f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Google antitrust case.
* Meta, TikTok and Snap say they oppose [Australia’s youth social media ban](https://substack.com/redirect/557c24e1-3662-430c-acc7-e1b6b028cc50?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) but will comply with it.
* Delaware attorney-general [warns of legal action](https://substack.com/redirect/44fce4f9-7f0a-4a7d-99ed-c65ec21b7e9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) if OpenAI fails to act in public interest.
* Here’s proof that those [iPhone typos](https://substack.com/redirect/62df56be-94b4-461b-bf16-051265a3787c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) you keep making aren’t your fault.

---

> *In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* 

> Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow *and* [prove](https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the ROI of brand spend.

> If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.

> [Tracksuit’s](https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll [get the budget](https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the next moderately chaotic idea.

---

* Microsoft reports [strong earnings](https://substack.com/redirect/0b3a64fd-c7a7-4f8f-bc31-7d430dd1daba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Azure hit by major outage.
	+ Microsoft just revealed that [OpenAI lost more than $11.5B](https://substack.com/redirect/19aaf252-3178-40e5-8f98-981fe231f2d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) last quarter.
		- [AI Spending](https://substack.com/redirect/6dc24cab-1394-4814-8d02-77a12403b420?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is what matters most in Alphabet, Microsoft earnings.
* UK ad spend forecast to [reach £12bn](https://substack.com/redirect/ad75f046-7607-433f-890f-ed20bee03f36?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) during this festive period.
* Here’s what [ads on your $2,000 Samsung smart fridge](https://substack.com/redirect/8ac7f97f-dd80-4c86-aeaf-b14464a96021?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will look like.
* [AOL](https://substack.com/redirect/0ac43016-3256-431e-9075-2012e9f0fa5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to be acquired by Italy’s Bending Spoons.
* China’s $143 billion push [to dominate](https://substack.com/redirect/b8b0304a-7024-42f0-829d-359cad9ab2d1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the global EV industry.
* The Hatred of Podcasting. ([read](https://substack.com/redirect/3aeccdc8-cfaf-4329-bf6f-1844205a834e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g))
* Netflix’s advertising tier has [almost half of its household viewing hours](https://substack.com/redirect/56307b71-d0ee-433c-a21c-9c1708c91807?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), according to [Comscore](https://substack.com/redirect/5b1192f9-c7e5-42d5-9fc6-15ddef3aa73d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* India’s [IPO engine runs hot](https://substack.com/redirect/f71535b0-90ef-4a13-9d9e-e7058711b9ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) despite some soft debuts.
	+ European IPO markets show [signs of revival](https://substack.com/redirect/96506548-2407-424c-85a8-f2d9e43f745b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* PepsiCo [overhauls](https://substack.com/redirect/7efebef7-784c-46ef-ae8c-5bbf300ae421?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) corporate branding to broaden focus beyond Pepsi.
* Oreo-maker Mondelez to use new generative AI tool to [slash marketing costs](https://substack.com/redirect/0c1ace50-c5ee-4ec3-ac2e-ec0cc4f0187b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* For [$20,000, a humanoid robot](https://substack.com/redirect/611407d5-6867-4acf-bdc5-8e5bb9ba10c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will do your household chores for you like unloading the dishwasher and watering plants—[but it still needs help](https://substack.com/redirect/53051949-a160-4886-942b-1d694f7bdbfd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* WPP CEO labels group’s performance ‘[unacceptable](https://substack.com/redirect/5677fff3-8a21-415b-a6d6-2f69f92e6822?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ as it looks to SMEs.
* [Fox News](https://substack.com/redirect/d537ce70-4670-4bc2-b964-ec76f23b0a1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reports [its highest first quarter ad revenue](https://substack.com/redirect/1d51dccd-f5e6-4a7b-89a3-0428cc7e33db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in history.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!BaXH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/ea73fc48-3504-4dc1-a6a3-113a6bb3400d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Apple is [about to](https://substack.com/redirect/847d98e2-8585-4694-b64a-8a4808915cf9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) plant ads inside [Apple Maps](https://substack.com/redirect/b579362c-5bce-41f7-9dc2-af347902e8ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* UK regulator [threatens](https://substack.com/redirect/b5dc105f-2927-441d-b976-0e23029345ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tech giants with [algorithm audits](https://substack.com/redirect/0e899a93-77a3-4f24-8e1b-6c9c2e6f544e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to protect children.
* Omnicom responds to rumors that it will [retire its DDB agency brand](https://substack.com/redirect/cb5da3ae-153b-4a05-8d95-4e421dff3a37?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* TikTok is not the leading social media platform for [Microdramas](https://substack.com/redirect/86888e64-cc5c-4525-b33d-bdf7099efff7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but you won’t be shocked by what is.
* ICE just [spent millions](https://substack.com/redirect/d3e079a1-0e59-4ed0-a8dc-c120be6952b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on a social media surveillance AI program, [Zignal Labs](https://substack.com/redirect/fd81fe3c-1d24-4d52-a275-6461da64b63a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ The Republican plan to [reform the census](https://substack.com/redirect/168c4801-77bb-450a-a2b3-8a8cfe3b3636?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) could put everyone’s privacy at risk.
* Nvidia hits [$5 trillion valuation](https://substack.com/redirect/733cd302-65c9-4f5d-9f7b-0bbfb4c4b362?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as AI boom powers meteoric rise.
* Uber launches a [platform-specific attention metric](https://substack.com/redirect/a73e3180-6b54-4488-9a05-e6ba634fdefe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) With Adelaide And Kantar.
* [Patreon](https://substack.com/redirect/4449a0a0-64b4-4a34-a4e3-d74d7d86987f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is trying to steal creators from Substack with their take on newsletters.
* [Rachel Karten](https://open.substack.com/users/8247620-rachel-karten?utm_source=mentions)’s Link in Bio publishes [The 2025 Social Media Salary Report](https://substack.com/redirect/38642734-ec83-46e9-be4c-0257da9cf31a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Inside an unprecedented settlement — why [Universal is ending its lawsuit](https://substack.com/redirect/5e36483f-32d5-4080-b2d5-52bd0dddaced?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) against AI-music platform Udio and partnering with it.
* Republicans claimed Biden censored [YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/337bed6e-57e0-4835-a590-e6bc6a52940f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). 20 employees seem to say otherwise.
* Adults are finally here to ruin/end the [‘6-7’ meme](https://substack.com/redirect/1869e1f5-242d-4073-82a7-aefb232ef85c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with their Halloween costumes.
* Paid armies of ‘[Clippers](https://substack.com/redirect/20ab0952-633c-402d-ac80-834a3f94c7b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ boost internet stars like MrBeast.
* Paramount [lays off](https://substack.com/redirect/c6719a09-e912-433e-8422-821788d7b81b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 1,000 workers in first round of cuts.
* Elon Musk challenges Wikipedia with [Grokpedia](https://substack.com/redirect/3c9c0ef5-9a91-4848-bfc6-6468f55d2850?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), which is full of [AI-cloned pages](https://substack.com/redirect/33b42043-dc75-4525-a4d0-5ff17cf80488?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from Wikipedia.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!sjUU!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa21cfcaf-a721-407c-a9db-778b1aff1e95_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/5830c294-7824-4604-b84a-aad58e3ef3d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* TikTok can [use AI](https://substack.com/redirect/b7954362-83be-4394-b5d7-1a65f8bf98be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to turn your long video into short ones.
* [TikTok creators](https://substack.com/redirect/26bb9d93-0d4d-4124-83d1-25de0ff81c60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can now get paid up to 90% of subscription revenue.
	+ [Three new features](https://substack.com/redirect/c7c86b0f-942d-48b3-a0da-5cfef6711ab5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and AI tools [announced](https://substack.com/redirect/8b93cab2-d946-40a6-bb2d-245d41ff6106?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at the TikTok U.S. Creator Summit.
* Nationals senator says [TikTok bullied his office](https://substack.com/redirect/3437cf68-1756-4b9e-9120-3d51d558fee4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid new teen social media ban details.
* Integral Ad Science and TikTok [extend](https://substack.com/redirect/a3965833-def2-4e58-87e1-b02f07f0771e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) brand safety partnership.
* TikTok inks [partnership](https://substack.com/redirect/bdc075cc-359b-43f3-aba9-efdc2a820b21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Vietnam’s Ministry of Culture, Sports and Tourism to boost digital ecosystem.
* TikTok is widely rolling out on iOS a new, [enhanced inbox](https://substack.com/redirect/69fbbd7e-94e4-45ea-81e2-61692396773f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for content creators.
* TikTok accused of ‘[backtracking](https://substack.com/redirect/a16038ee-1052-4d72-89ed-d6d9f2c449b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ on safety commitments.
* TikTok experimenting with [ability to disable HDR videos](https://substack.com/redirect/c6d899e2-4490-4e34-b132-42bffcd3a980?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), converting them to SDR, and an option to hide video tags for a cleaner viewing experience.
* What TikTok’s ‘[Bird Theory](https://substack.com/redirect/000a9771-0c87-4895-aec0-88276c50a2b7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ says about relationships.
* Hybristophilia: Study links TikTok to [women’s sexual attraction](https://substack.com/redirect/a50db183-7b90-4b13-bbc9-bad73be2cc9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to criminals.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dE8f!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/9d28cede-fd86-4315-9596-c417c246301d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Meta [introduces](https://substack.com/redirect/d2818e73-395e-4bcf-8232-ca5f47039dd3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Disappearing posts on Threads.
	+ Would you [ghost post](https://substack.com/redirect/1904993c-76ad-43bc-8c3b-bfa8786a8a12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Threads?
* On Meta earnings call, Mark Zuckerberg claims Reels “has an [annual run rate](https://substack.com/redirect/d36a2549-2a66-471c-9bff-0da479c7aad9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of over $50 billion.”
* Threads adding ‘[Reply Approvals](https://substack.com/redirect/5887740d-13ab-4bd0-bff9-90b680288e3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to combat online trolls and spam.
* Instagram now allows users to [attach stickers](https://substack.com/redirect/2a96093f-dd05-4156-a9a4-721b6781ab00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to private text messages.
* Instagram is starting to [publicly test](https://substack.com/redirect/342d1dbf-9319-44e5-9e01-4eb4bae0bf85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the ability to see and control your algorithm.
* Threads reaches [150 million daily users](https://substack.com/redirect/6e9e004d-bb5a-44e5-94e2-60addb652ba4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and is ramping up ads.
* Instagram is working on [Games](https://substack.com/redirect/3f57be52-39cf-4c4d-b28c-f9dc358d9236?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for DMs.
* Threads to show “Online” user status and members count for [Communities](https://substack.com/redirect/2da46f26-2065-4c1c-8dd7-9d7e67747a3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Snowflake says exec shared unauthorized guidance in [Instagram street interview](https://substack.com/redirect/1d29bd20-8e7f-41d7-9057-acf52b7d42c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🪑 Report Break: According to WPP Media’s *[How Humans Decide](https://substack.com/redirect/2b6347ca-5875-4969-b79f-12306b866203?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* whitepaper, produced with Oxford University’s Saïd Business School, 84% of purchases involve people choosing brands they’re *already* biased towards. 

> This leaves just 16% of sales open to influence through lower-funnel marketing, making long-term brand building more crucial than ever. 

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VXYk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/a77adcc4-86a8-4002-a12b-e71514981fb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Meta’s [price per ad up 10%](https://substack.com/redirect/4cb02fc8-4c9c-4ed9-90b1-74925e1ee2ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), overall ad revenue surged 26% to $51.24 billion in Q3 2025.
* Meta announces new updates to [Lead Generation ads](https://substack.com/redirect/b226bb4b-c8e3-459d-979f-51f9960b6a2d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Q5 Marketing.
* Zuckerberg [appoints](https://substack.com/redirect/e1a6b8fd-b240-4282-89d2-dd2edbf0a3f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) former metaverse executive to boost AI efforts.
	+ He is excited to add [more AI content](https://substack.com/redirect/edb79ea9-d616-4751-8401-ccc174792f1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to all your social feeds.
* WhatsApp can now use [passkeys to secure](https://substack.com/redirect/1cab174c-bb68-49be-ad5e-c5e7220f261e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) your backups.
* Meta shares [new insights](https://substack.com/redirect/827bc137-d8fa-48d2-87fd-f6e9ca6cf523?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Marketing Mix Modeling strategies and practices.
* Facebook [simplifies](https://substack.com/redirect/5e3fac13-b486-4a79-ac73-5a149b243b63?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) page creation process.
* Meta’s [Creative Similarity Score](https://substack.com/redirect/b5e7a270-ea5d-444b-a35e-e5a6aebef128?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) helps [advertisers](https://substack.com/redirect/56337492-356a-4de0-9af8-a840e0469a97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) improve CTR and reduce ad creative fatigue.
* Meta, xAI [starting trend for billions](https://substack.com/redirect/fc054785-ce62-469e-814d-10d659a058b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in off-balance sheet debt.
	+ Meta has an [AI product](https://substack.com/redirect/e7f63a94-d68c-4f00-8ce3-13c54bd05db9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) problem.
* Meta denies [torrenting porn](https://substack.com/redirect/6f8e04ed-c2ed-44d6-aeb3-2bdd05f43e53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to train AI, says downloads were for “personal use”.
* WhatsApp [testing](https://substack.com/redirect/55dd144a-1522-43e5-a439-7d994c092709?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Apple Watch app.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xdrk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/2f8805a9-f285-42f2-947b-bfcdf6a6deb4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* YouTube Advertising hits [$10.3 billion](https://substack.com/redirect/7763e5c2-ea8c-4fd7-b838-c55db59bd408?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as it takes growing share of TV market.
* Shorts now make [more money for YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/0cdbcc26-5d96-4e17-a118-b555bcfec380?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) than long-form videos.
* YouTube launches [new features](https://substack.com/redirect/474e450e-b55f-4282-95e8-4709abe7793d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to improve user and creator experience on TV.
* Disney pulls channels from [YouTube TV](https://substack.com/redirect/3bbb5003-6de5-46b8-b96b-3d37b5371942?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over fee dispute.
	+ Disney and YouTube [settle lawsuit](https://substack.com/redirect/080c6b30-f862-404a-8ba1-70287492909f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over poaching of Exec Justin Connolly.
	+ YouTube TV [flexes muscles again](https://substack.com/redirect/05f8ccd8-2ae7-4a90-8cc2-1c7317b95722?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in showdown with Disney.
* YouTube [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/fc776932-c89a-42fd-9bfd-52b5832279ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ability to hide or opt-out of the Top Fan Leaderboard for Classic and Immersive Live Streams.
* YouTube [updates](https://substack.com/redirect/c74a2d88-dd58-49db-9b91-6b190c2eebb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) restrictions on gaming and gambling content.
* YouTube [renames](https://substack.com/redirect/fc776932-c89a-42fd-9bfd-52b5832279ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Copyright Tab to Content Detection in YouTube Studio.
* YouTube TV inks [Bloomberg media deal](https://substack.com/redirect/036a8897-6d13-4878-aeb4-529b989b7d41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for 24-hour news and originals channels.
* YouTube pushed [a highly NSFW thumbnail](https://substack.com/redirect/ab91fe51-cd6e-4fbd-abdd-4daeb9daa6f2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to millions of unsuspecting users.
* YouTube will let you [opt out of AI upscaling](https://substack.com/redirect/f42347f8-258d-40d6-8ae5-7f0ca7ef2a66?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on low-res videos.
* Adobe and YouTube launch ‘[Create for YouTube Shorts](https://substack.com/redirect/2e84699a-8d28-4118-9184-3f4b142c4a2b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ in Premiere Mobile.
* YouTube is offering [employees buyouts](https://substack.com/redirect/f7be3e89-3ae0-4083-8934-514368ccd7da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as part of an AI-focused reorganization.
* YouTube will upgrade video and thumbnail file size limits ‘[soon](https://substack.com/redirect/a70573c0-6ae4-4959-aa09-351f4d2d95e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’.
* YouTube denies AI was involved with [odd removals](https://substack.com/redirect/34e24232-29d7-4350-b1ab-a76df7b22b85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of tech tutorials.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!SRmL!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6ddeabac-2fb9-4449-8c6d-6f3b735dce87_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/37bb4cd5-7b25-44fe-8642-fa0c887ab90a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Google shows [first market share rebound](https://substack.com/redirect/a9141e39-cb74-4445-9517-514649693ac4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) since AI Search surge. While the increase seems modest, from 90.54% to 90.71%, it amounts to billions in potential revenue as AI-powered challengers start chipping away at the remaining 10%.
	+ In data that BrightEdge shared with Fast Company in advance of publishing the new findings, Google had [lost 1.5% of the search market share](https://substack.com/redirect/bad63e13-bc67-4ac9-81de-7193013f4110?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2025.
* Google promotes [fake content](https://substack.com/redirect/7dca0f6d-713e-4c2e-a518-e7e1252ec2f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to millions on Discover news platform.
* Google Labs & DeepMind launch [Pomelli AI](https://substack.com/redirect/111c0aa2-80f2-4c0f-986d-bfa20da499b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) marketing tool.
* Google partners with [Ambani’s Reliance](https://substack.com/redirect/073cde5d-53b6-41cd-9cca-d56706ac1cb3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to offer free AI Pro access to millions of Jio users in India.
* Google Search Console [adds](https://substack.com/redirect/1f0cfea1-6fb9-4049-bbe3-790285e689f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Query groups.
* Adobe and Google Cloud [expand](https://substack.com/redirect/383a5fe0-cb75-4f59-8e0f-1dd16dcd20a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) strategic partnership.

> 🥀 Report Break: More than 9% of all news in U.S. newspapers contains at least some text created by artificial intelligence (AI), according to [new research](https://substack.com/redirect/bed38995-7632-4cde-bff0-49675acd984c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) led by University of Maryland computer scientists.

> The team examined 186,000 articles published by 1,500 newspapers in the summer of 2025 and found that 9.1% were either [AI-generated or mixed](https://substack.com/redirect/133eeb60-e380-4c5f-8c26-c96931746e4b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). Only 1.7% of articles at papers with circulation of more than 100,000 were partially or fully AI-generated. But at smaller ones, nearly 1 in 10 (9.3%) was shown to contain significant AI content.

	+ [G2 and Reddit](https://substack.com/redirect/edc6fa32-e139-4a41-9d1f-8b7f10a1b6a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partner to help B2B companies expand their trusted presence to new communities.

* [New research](https://substack.com/redirect/8642b8fd-02a6-4a04-bd1d-e9b7736eb304?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from Snapchat, NRG and Stagwell Group show how gift giving isn’t just about birthdays and holidays anymore.

> 🧃 Report Break: Weight loss drugs are bringing down [the country’s obesity rate](https://substack.com/redirect/cd903457-25aa-4bca-a357-83c1bc8b60bc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a survey shows. 

> After peaking at a record high of 39.9% in 2022, the U.S. adult obesity rate has gradually declined to 37.0% in 2025. This is a statistically meaningful decrease representing an estimated 7.6 million fewer obese adults compared with three years ago.

###### Spending & Life: 87% of consumers plan to shop at discount retailers this season, according to [Bank of America](https://substack.com/redirect/3be4e9c3-7482-4273-bd72-9539ae1289cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!vjWG!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd95f9eb0-763c-4b18-ab9d-fcdb1b8f86e5_1500x649.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/f15e3429-d245-45cb-b740-f2ff10ac2478?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)
> [VISA](https://substack.com/redirect/0d330229-07d8-4c2b-a9e1-ee6a020fb2c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): With inflation on holiday-related items higher this year than last season, nominally reported holiday sales are expected to rise 4.6% year-over-year, up from a 4.3% pace last year.

> After accounting for inflation on holiday items, real spending — a good proxy for foot traffic — is expected to rise just 2.2% this year, down from the 2.5% real spending that occurred last season.

> The [2.2% real spending growth](https://substack.com/redirect/0d330229-07d8-4c2b-a9e1-ee6a020fb2c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) means fewer items are being purchased, even as dollar volumes increase, suggesting potential challenges for inventory turnover and margin management.

* ITN and Magnite partner to allow [programmatic access](https://substack.com/redirect/211f91a9-c82b-450b-a08b-6baf145421b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Local Linear TV.
* StackAdapt [debuts](https://substack.com/redirect/1dfb4a58-17e9-4199-952a-c58147a913bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) map-first DOOH planning.

> 🥀 Report Break: Experian’s analysis based on over 1.4 billion anonymised transactions across more than 4,000 retail brands reveals that [Black Friday’s importance as a single day has decreased](https://substack.com/redirect/f26e50a6-4075-4418-ba4d-456994a49cb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) when compared to the broader trading window\* - which increased by more than 10% compared to 2023.

> The value-driven retail (+42% year-on-year), resale marketplace platforms (+21% year-on-year) and social shopping channels (+23.6% year-on-year) are thriving. This growth is particularly strong among the 65+ age group\*\*, who now account for 14% of total online spend, highlighting how people, and especially older demographics, are seeking value, sustainability and convenience.

---

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e937f2e-6613-43f8-80fd-0ba402577997?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/95f0b1fb-3469-4ae2-aad1-ce6cc5c3c0bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/969213ad-c1eb-4577-8007-d7c9499a94f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":50683,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VaNH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Canva takes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d07fa673-aecf-46c3-bd92-2e0fc6d7bc1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">direct aim</a><span> at Adobe’s egregious prices and moves </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e771546-dcb4-4d48-ad07-42b85e0f0f27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Affinity</a><span> to a free model.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Trump-Xi meeting proves </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/260b3c9a-f992-4dd6-b3de-f8b7ed09356a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">less than pivotal</a><span> for TikTok deal.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Neither President Trump nor Chinese officials indicated any new developments for the popular video app.</p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Amazon ad revenue </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b2891ea-0b77-4ae1-b0c1-31170fb0de3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">soars 24%</a><span> to $17.7 billion, net revenue increased 13% to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fd2352eb-b09d-466e-b12f-36399e48226b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$180 billion</a><span> in Q3, AWS revenue jumps 20%.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amazon confirms plans to lay off </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9a327866-c250-46b7-8c39-e0eaa1d997d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">14,000 corporate workers</a><span> as part of wave of cuts.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>OpenAI gives </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/abaad365-a107-42d7-aeec-86a9657a99eb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Microsoft 27% stake</a><span>, completes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/82ef577f-b4cb-4cdb-96c7-ea82a2936de1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">for-profit</a><span> shift.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI has an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/36368774-fb3b-4ecd-ab40-ac3aa2f36fc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AGI problem</a><span> — and Microsoft just made it worse.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5cf9981c-efd3-4615-bdda-fc71fac415f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Netflix</a><span> is actively </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dd9979fc-57d1-4988-ad5b-6591aef02211?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">exploring</a><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dd9979fc-57d1-4988-ad5b-6591aef02211?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a bid</a><span> for Warner Bros. Discovery Inc.’s studio and streaming businesses.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta brings in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25021085-6ed3-4f98-b975-ba37297bc04c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">record quarterly revenue</a><span> as Q3 sales rise 26% YoY, but </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/716734b4-3aa7-4314-aea9-76a1c0eb65fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a major tax bill</a><span> dampens earnings per share.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a93aff30-fb67-4100-bd89-8ab85e214f27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google ads hit </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e8a0e94-7a2c-44f9-806a-fac4444522c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$74.18 bn</a><span> as Alphabet crosses </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/080be7d6-b413-4164-8fc0-b41e62cb09be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$100 bn barrier</a><span> in Q3 2025.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Pinterest launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a031de02-a997-4b29-ae14-1c768c1de09d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new AI Assistant</a><span> to enhance online shopping experience &amp; product discovery.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f2225478-222b-421d-bad5-660262c8e714?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Adobe Max 2025</a><span>: all the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aef9abea-f845-4c2c-8c67-5a6f7e97efd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">latest creative tools</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/62a51662-693c-4fd4-a918-3dcbfb4b9738?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI announcements</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8161fdbd-2aef-4b0c-b0f0-65147c2abb30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> AI assistants for Express and Photoshop.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe now lets you </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c6541fe2-6b71-42bc-b412-575475cfc398?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">generate</a><span> soundtracks and speech in Firefly.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Reddit’s sales </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f9729e8f-d087-4120-a19d-0896ff25a330?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">jumped 68% YoY to $585 million</a><span> in the third quarter, Daily Active Uniques increased 19% YoY to 116.0 million.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>U.S. Senators announce bill that would </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/05f3cf93-b735-489a-a27d-988762205006?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ban AI chatbot companions</a><span> for minors.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Character.AI bans </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/42924f80-31d0-4714-a620-7a18c3fe2dac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">users under 18</a><span> after being sued over child’s suicide.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Roblox shares drop after saying plans to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4bf47bc5-e0dc-42c2-a071-e5b24615fa34?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prioritize safety impact</a><span> may weigh on growth next year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Judge rules for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3dda27e4-5afa-434b-9f84-cb287a1037f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publishers</a><span> in Google antitrust case.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta, TikTok and Snap say they oppose </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/557c24e1-3662-430c-acc7-e1b6b028cc50?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Australia’s youth social media ban</a><span> but will comply with it.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Delaware attorney-general </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/44fce4f9-7f0a-4a7d-99ed-c65ec21b7e9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">warns of legal action</a><span> if OpenAI fails to act in public interest.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Here’s proof that those </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/62df56be-94b4-461b-bf16-051265a3787c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">iPhone typos</a><span> you keep making aren’t your fault.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> </span></em></p></blockquote>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow </span><em>and</em><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prove</a><span> the ROI of brand spend.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit’s </a><span>always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fe70b2a3-14de-40dc-bfd7-31db71f6967f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">get the budget</a><span> for the next moderately chaotic idea.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Microsoft reports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0b3a64fd-c7a7-4f8f-bc31-7d430dd1daba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">strong earnings</a><span> as Azure hit by major outage.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Microsoft just revealed that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/19aaf252-3178-40e5-8f98-981fe231f2d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI lost more than $11.5B</a><span> last quarter.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6dc24cab-1394-4814-8d02-77a12403b420?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Spending</a><span> is what matters most in Alphabet, Microsoft earnings.</span></p></li></ul>
</li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>UK ad spend forecast to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad75f046-7607-433f-890f-ed20bee03f36?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reach £12bn</a><span> during this festive period.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Here’s what </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8ac7f97f-dd80-4c86-aeaf-b14464a96021?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ads on your $2,000 Samsung smart fridge </a><span>will look like.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0ac43016-3256-431e-9075-2012e9f0fa5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AOL</a><span> to be acquired by Italy’s Bending Spoons.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>China’s $143 billion push </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b8b0304a-7024-42f0-829d-359cad9ab2d1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to dominate</a><span> the global EV industry.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Hatred of Podcasting. (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3aeccdc8-cfaf-4329-bf6f-1844205a834e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">read</a><span>)</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Netflix’s advertising tier has </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/56307b71-d0ee-433c-a21c-9c1708c91807?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">almost half of its household viewing hours</a><span>, according to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b1192f9-c7e5-42d5-9fc6-15ddef3aa73d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Comscore</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>India’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f71535b0-90ef-4a13-9d9e-e7058711b9ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IPO engine runs hot</a><span> despite some soft debuts.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>European IPO markets show </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/96506548-2407-424c-85a8-f2d9e43f745b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">signs of revival</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>PepsiCo </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7efebef7-784c-46ef-ae8c-5bbf300ae421?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">overhauls</a><span> corporate branding to broaden focus beyond Pepsi.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Oreo-maker Mondelez to use new generative AI tool to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0c1ace50-c5ee-4ec3-ac2e-ec0cc4f0187b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">slash marketing costs</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>For </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/611407d5-6867-4acf-bdc5-8e5bb9ba10c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$20,000, a humanoid robot</a><span> will do your household chores for you like unloading the dishwasher and watering plants—</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/53051949-a160-4886-942b-1d694f7bdbfd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">but it still needs help</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WPP CEO labels group’s performance ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5677fff3-8a21-415b-a6d6-2f69f92e6822?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unacceptable</a><span>’ as it looks to SMEs.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d537ce70-4670-4bc2-b964-ec76f23b0a1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Fox News</a><span> reports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1d51dccd-f5e6-4a7b-89a3-0428cc7e33db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">its highest first quarter ad revenue</a><span> in history.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea73fc48-3504-4dc1-a6a3-113a6bb3400d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":53060,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!BaXH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/847d98e2-8585-4694-b64a-8a4808915cf9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">about to</a><span> plant ads inside </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b579362c-5bce-41f7-9dc2-af347902e8ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple Maps</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>UK regulator </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b5dc105f-2927-441d-b976-0e23029345ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">threatens</a><span> tech giants with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0e899a93-77a3-4f24-8e1b-6c9c2e6f544e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">algorithm audits</a><span> to protect children.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Omnicom responds to rumors that it will </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cb5da3ae-153b-4a05-8d95-4e421dff3a37?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">retire its DDB agency brand</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok is not the leading social media platform for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/86888e64-cc5c-4525-b33d-bdf7099efff7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Microdramas</a><span>, but you won’t be shocked by what is.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>ICE just </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d3e079a1-0e59-4ed0-a8dc-c120be6952b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">spent millions</a><span> on a social media surveillance AI program, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fd81fe3c-1d24-4d52-a275-6461da64b63a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Zignal Labs</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Republican plan to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/168c4801-77bb-450a-a2b3-8a8cfe3b3636?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reform the census</a><span> could put everyone’s privacy at risk. </span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nvidia hits </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/733cd302-65c9-4f5d-9f7b-0bbfb4c4b362?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$5 trillion valuation</a><span> as AI boom powers meteoric rise.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Uber launches a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a73e3180-6b54-4488-9a05-e6ba634fdefe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">platform-specific attention metric</a><span> With Adelaide And Kantar.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4449a0a0-64b4-4a34-a4e3-d74d7d86987f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Patreon</a><span> is trying to steal creators from Substack with their take on newsletters.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span data-component-name="MentionStatic" style="white-space: nowrap;cursor: pointer;color: #e85c57;border-radius: 4px;transition: color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),background-color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),box-shadow .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),opacity .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),filter .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),stroke .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),transform .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),scale .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),outline .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1);margin: 0 -.125em;padding: 0 .125em .0625em;text-decoration: none;"><a href="https://open.substack.com/users/8247620-rachel-karten?utm\_source=mentions" style="-webkit-text-decoration: none;color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Rachel Karten</a></span><span>’s Link in Bio publishes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/38642734-ec83-46e9-be4c-0257da9cf31a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The 2025 Social Media Salary Report</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Inside an unprecedented settlement — why </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5e36483f-32d5-4080-b2d5-52bd0dddaced?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Universal is ending its lawsuit</a><span> against AI-music platform Udio and partnering with it.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Republicans claimed Biden censored </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/337bed6e-57e0-4835-a590-e6bc6a52940f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">YouTube</a><span>. 20 employees seem to say otherwise.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adults are finally here to ruin/end the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1869e1f5-242d-4073-82a7-aefb232ef85c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">‘6-7’ meme</a><span> with their Halloween costumes.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Paid armies of ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/20ab0952-633c-402d-ac80-834a3f94c7b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Clippers</a><span>’ boost internet stars like MrBeast.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Paramount </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c6719a09-e912-433e-8422-821788d7b81b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lays off</a><span> 1,000 workers in first round of cuts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk challenges Wikipedia with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3c9c0ef5-9a91-4848-bfc6-6468f55d2850?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Grokpedia</a><span>, which is full of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/33b42043-dc75-4525-a4d0-5ff17cf80488?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-cloned pages</a><span> from Wikipedia.</span></p></li>
</ul>
