<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b7954362-83be-4394-b5d7-1a65f8bf98be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">use AI</a><span> to turn your long video into short ones.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/26bb9d93-0d4d-4124-83d1-25de0ff81c60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok creators</a><span> can now get paid up to 90% of subscription revenue.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c7c86b0f-942d-48b3-a0da-5cfef6711ab5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Three new features</a><span> and AI tools </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8b93cab2-d946-40a6-bb2d-245d41ff6106?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">announced</a><span> at the TikTok U.S. Creator Summit.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nationals senator says </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3437cf68-1756-4b9e-9120-3d51d558fee4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok bullied his office</a><span> amid new teen social media ban details.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Integral Ad Science and TikTok </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a3965833-def2-4e58-87e1-b02f07f0771e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">extend</a><span> brand safety partnership.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok inks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bdc075cc-359b-43f3-aba9-efdc2a820b21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">partnership</a><span> with Vietnam’s Ministry of Culture, Sports and Tourism to boost digital ecosystem.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok is widely rolling out on iOS a new, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/69fbbd7e-94e4-45ea-81e2-61692396773f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">enhanced inbox</a><span> for content creators.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok accused of ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a16038ee-1052-4d72-89ed-d6d9f2c449b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">backtracking</a><span>’ on safety commitments.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok experimenting with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c6d899e2-4490-4e34-b132-42bffcd3a980?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ability to disable HDR videos</a><span>, converting them to SDR, and an option to hide video tags for a cleaner viewing experience.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>What TikTok’s ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/000a9771-0c87-4895-aec0-88276c50a2b7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bird Theory</a><span>’ says about relationships.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Hybristophilia: Study links TikTok to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a50db183-7b90-4b13-bbc9-bad73be2cc9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">women’s sexual attraction</a><span> to criminals.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d2818e73-395e-4bcf-8232-ca5f47039dd3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">introduces</a><span> Disappearing posts on Threads.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Would you </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1904993c-76ad-43bc-8c3b-bfa8786a8a12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ghost post</a><span> on Threads?</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>On Meta earnings call, Mark Zuckerberg claims Reels “has an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d36a2549-2a66-471c-9bff-0da479c7aad9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">annual run rate </a><span>of over $50 billion.”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads adding ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5887740d-13ab-4bd0-bff9-90b680288e3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reply Approvals</a><span>’ to combat online trolls and spam. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram now allows users to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a96093f-dd05-4156-a9a4-721b6781ab00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">attach stickers</a><span> to private text messages.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is starting to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/342d1dbf-9319-44e5-9e01-4eb4bae0bf85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publicly test</a><span> the ability to see and control your algorithm.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads reaches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e9e004d-bb5a-44e5-94e2-60addb652ba4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">150 million daily users</a><span> and is ramping up ads.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is working on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3f57be52-39cf-4c4d-b28c-f9dc358d9236?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Games</a><span> for DMs. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads to show “Online” user status and members count for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2da46f26-2065-4c1c-8dd7-9d7e67747a3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Communities</a><span>. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Snowflake says exec shared unauthorized guidance in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1d29bd20-8e7f-41d7-9057-acf52b7d42c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram street interview</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🪑 Report Break: According to WPP Media’s </span><em><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2b6347ca-5875-4969-b79f-12306b866203?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">How Humans Decide</a></em><span> whitepaper, produced with Oxford University’s Saïd Business School, 84% of purchases involve people choosing brands they’re </span><em>already </em><span>biased towards. </span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">This leaves just 16% of sales open to influence through lower-funnel marketing, making long-term brand building more crucial than ever. </p>
</blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4cb02fc8-4c9c-4ed9-90b1-74925e1ee2ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">price per ad up 10%</a><span>, overall ad revenue surged 26% to $51.24 billion in Q3 2025. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta announces new updates to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b226bb4b-c8e3-459d-979f-51f9960b6a2d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lead Generation ads</a><span> for Q5 Marketing.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Zuckerberg </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e1a6b8fd-b240-4282-89d2-dd2edbf0a3f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">appoints</a><span> former metaverse executive to boost AI efforts.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>He is excited to add </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/edb79ea9-d616-4751-8401-ccc174792f1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more AI content</a><span> to all your social feeds.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp can now use </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1cab174c-bb68-49be-ad5e-c5e7220f261e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">passkeys to secure</a><span> your backups.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta shares </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/827bc137-d8fa-48d2-87fd-f6e9ca6cf523?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new insights</a><span> on Marketing Mix Modeling strategies and practices.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Facebook </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5e3fac13-b486-4a79-ac73-5a149b243b63?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">simplifies</a><span> page creation process.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b5e7a270-ea5d-444b-a35e-e5a6aebef128?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Creative Similarity Score</a><span> helps </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/56337492-356a-4de0-9af8-a840e0469a97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertisers</a><span> improve CTR and reduce ad creative fatigue.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Meta, xAI </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc054785-ce62-469e-814d-10d659a058b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">starting trend for billions</a><span> in off-balance sheet debt.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta has an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e7f63a94-d68c-4f00-8ce3-13c54bd05db9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI product</a><span> problem.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta denies </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6f8e04ed-c2ed-44d6-aeb3-2bdd05f43e53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">torrenting porn</a><span> to train AI, says downloads were for “personal use”.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/55dd144a-1522-43e5-a439-7d994c092709?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">testing</a><span> Apple Watch app.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube Advertising hits </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7763e5c2-ea8c-4fd7-b838-c55db59bd408?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$10.3 billion</a><span> as it takes growing share of TV market.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Shorts now make </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0cdbcc26-5d96-4e17-a118-b555bcfec380?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more money for YouTube</a><span> than long-form videos.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/474e450e-b55f-4282-95e8-4709abe7793d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new features</a><span> to improve user and creator experience on TV.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Disney pulls channels from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3bbb5003-6de5-46b8-b96b-3d37b5371942?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">YouTube TV</a><span> over fee dispute.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Disney and YouTube </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/080c6b30-f862-404a-8ba1-70287492909f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">settle lawsuit</a><span> over poaching of Exec Justin Connolly.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube TV </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/05f8ccd8-2ae7-4a90-8cc2-1c7317b95722?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">flexes muscles again</a><span> in showdown with Disney.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc776932-c89a-42fd-9bfd-52b5832279ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> ability to hide or opt-out of the Top Fan Leaderboard for Classic and Immersive Live Streams.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c74a2d88-dd58-49db-9b91-6b190c2eebb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">updates</a><span> restrictions on gaming and gambling content.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc776932-c89a-42fd-9bfd-52b5832279ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">renames</a><span> Copyright Tab to Content Detection in YouTube Studio.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube TV inks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/036a8897-6d13-4878-aeb4-529b989b7d41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bloomberg media deal</a><span> for 24-hour news and originals channels.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube pushed </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ab91fe51-cd6e-4fbd-abdd-4daeb9daa6f2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a highly NSFW thumbnail</a><span> to millions of unsuspecting users.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube will let you</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f42347f8-258d-40d6-8ae5-7f0ca7ef2a66?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> opt out of AI upscaling</a><span> on low-res videos.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe and YouTube launch ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e84699a-8d28-4118-9184-3f4b142c4a2b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Create for YouTube Shorts</a><span>’ in Premiere Mobile.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube is offering </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f7be3e89-3ae0-4083-8934-514368ccd7da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">employees buyouts</a><span> as part of an AI-focused reorganization.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube will upgrade video and thumbnail file size limits ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a70573c0-6ae4-4959-aa09-351f4d2d95e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">soon</a><span>’.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube denies AI was involved with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/34e24232-29d7-4350-b1ab-a76df7b22b85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">odd removals</a><span> of tech tutorials.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Google shows </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a9141e39-cb74-4445-9517-514649693ac4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">first market share rebound</a><span> since AI Search surge. While the increase seems modest, from 90.54% to 90.71%, it amounts to billions in potential revenue as AI-powered challengers start chipping away at the remaining 10%.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>In data that BrightEdge shared with Fast Company in advance of publishing the new findings, Google had </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bad63e13-bc67-4ac9-81de-7193013f4110?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lost 1.5% of the search market share</a><span> in 2025.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google promotes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7dca0f6d-713e-4c2e-a518-e7e1252ec2f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fake content</a><span> to millions on Discover news platform.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Labs &amp; DeepMind launch </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/111c0aa2-80f2-4c0f-986d-bfa20da499b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pomelli AI</a><span> marketing tool.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google partners with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/073cde5d-53b6-41cd-9cca-d56706ac1cb3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ambani’s Reliance</a><span> to offer free AI Pro access to millions of Jio users in India.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Search Console </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f0cfea1-6fb9-4049-bbe3-790285e689f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">adds</a><span> Query groups.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe and Google Cloud </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/383a5fe0-cb75-4f59-8e0f-1dd16dcd20a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expand</a><span> strategic partnership.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/61f43205-3fe0-4b9c-9d63-3133d067811b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google Q3 Report</a><span>: AI Mode, AI Overviews lift total search usage.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Business Profiles </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d13cb97c-62ae-4ffc-b416-daa648a53d58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">What’s happening feature</a><span> expands. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8b78011d-e27e-4d20-9fca-066cea0ad3fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">first AI ad</a><span> avoids the uncanny valley by casting a Turkey.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google makes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3983a03f-1a20-4fd0-b8d5-de0b170d9d0c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">first Play Store changes</a><span> after losing Epic Games antitrust case.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google plans to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ba71fae7-a608-413d-8a45-6f1432e3845a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expand Opal</a><span> with advanced Agent capabilities.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google’s Robby Stein: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/46b09af7-b82d-4c3c-a83b-d9e56bc6c2ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ads aren’t going away</a><span> in AI search.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/265eeb58-42e7-4ae2-8edb-1da7ef5d2509?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI tool</a><span> to assist SMBs in creating brand promotions.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Chrome to warn users before </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a0b9a927-c5d8-4193-a96a-79d2ce762dc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">loading HTTP sites</a><span> starting next year.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X reports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b4802be9-cf17-4717-b174-dfb7aebf1ce7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">higher usage in EU</a><span> in latest DSA report.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Report: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/46727972-ac09-43a7-af71-8b7c872890c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple</a><span> Satellite Partner Globalstar could sell to SpaceX.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk’s xAI </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/11584877-6b9b-494a-a9e3-4c02edaf1af3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">leases</a><span> massive Palo Alto office near HQs.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>OpenAI’s Sam Altman and Musk clash again on X: “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a4ce4c3b-a944-4061-b38c-10bd8dbf1019?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">You stole a non-profit.</a><span>”</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk wants you to know that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/624da29e-564d-47d1-976c-5701d90cae7d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sam Altman</a><span> got a refund from Tesla.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X’s Grokipedia is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6de4bbf6-276b-435d-89dd-271df39d3531?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">online</a><span> after it briefly crashed out.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X to soon add a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fcea39ee-e628-4733-8945-8b958712e0fe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">“curated Following” tab</a><span>, semantic search and other updates to Grok. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X assures passkey reset is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/98d98e04-9f0c-42b6-a719-e252cfd4ad3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">nothing to worry</a><span> about.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X’s upcoming “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5711a242-1f55-44cc-b3c5-d68b7e724ede?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">About This Account</a><span>” feature will be allow users to choose between showing your country or showing your region.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Musk’s political foray cost Tesla huge sales, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/595a7b81-bb00-4b89-83bd-4715923923d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Yale study</a><span> finds.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X adds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/93373728-2272-4efc-8ba9-6ff7f10d2ea2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a dedicated tab</a><span> for message requests.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🥀 Report Break: More than 9% of all news in U.S. newspapers contains at least some text created by artificial intelligence (AI), according to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bed38995-7632-4cde-bff0-49675acd984c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new research</a><span> led by University of Maryland computer scientists.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The team examined 186,000 articles published by 1,500 newspapers in the summer of 2025 and found that 9.1% were either </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/133eeb60-e380-4c5f-8c26-c96931746e4b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated or mixed</a><span>. Only 1.7% of articles at papers with circulation of more than 100,000 were partially or fully AI-generated. But at smaller ones, nearly 1 in 10 (9.3%) was shown to contain significant AI content.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ff7317d3-cd0f-4dda-a1fa-b19cfca9e7c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reddit CEO</a><span> says chatbots are not a traffic driver.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/edc6fa32-e139-4a41-9d1f-8b7f10a1b6a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">G2 and Reddit</a><span> partner to help B2B companies expand their trusted presence to new communities.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Reddit is already in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f2a834e9-720a-44df-a1ee-4bf3fcc133b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">early testing</a><span> with its own AI campaign tool.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>SerpAPI calls </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/58f63aeb-f75c-4536-8c9a-0fb04be45f68?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reddit lawsuit</a><span> a threat to the ‘free and open web’.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8642b8fd-02a6-4a04-bd1d-e9b7736eb304?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New research</a><span> from Snapchat, NRG and Stagwell Group show how gift giving isn’t just about birthdays and holidays anymore.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Gifting isn’t just for others. 71% of consumers celebrate their own accomplishments, and 70% purchase items for themselves to mark small moments.</p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Snapchat tests “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/660963df-3254-4eed-9a4f-1e7d2404664b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Buddy Pass</a><span>” for Plus subscribers, allowing them to share 7 days of free Snapchat+ membership with up to 3 friends.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Netflix is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5664ee16-f40f-40d2-b8bb-1f7e97047011?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">adding</a><span> three new app features, here’s what’s coming.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>What’s with the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc85c47b-da1c-41f7-89b7-da62035ba902?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sponcon slop</a><span> in Nobody Wants This? Netflix, nobody wants this.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8b201596-68ba-4aac-b8ba-fb37393e5c16?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Firefly Image 5</a><span> brings support for layers, will let creators make custom models.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>News Corp-owned U.K. tabloid </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3e6059c0-e9cd-4ee7-a438-b1402c0f823c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Sun is building an AI agent</a><span> for its programmatic business.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Roku sees </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/11ff6990-401c-45cd-8fd2-1f4af2bb15df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">operating income</a><span> turn Positive for first time since 2021.</span></p></li>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🧃 Report Break: Weight loss drugs are bringing down </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cd903457-25aa-4bca-a357-83c1bc8b60bc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">the country’s obesity rate</a><span>, a survey shows. </span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">After peaking at a record high of 39.9% in 2022, the U.S. adult obesity rate has gradually declined to 37.0% in 2025. This is a statistically meaningful decrease representing an estimated 7.6 million fewer obese adults compared with three years ago.</p>
</blockquote>

<h4 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.125em;">
<span>Spending &amp; Life: 87% of consumers plan to shop at discount retailers this season, according to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3be4e9c3-7482-4273-bd72-9539ae1289cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">Bank of America</a><span>. </span>
</h4>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="712"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f15e3429-d245-45cb-b740-f2ff10ac2478?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/d95f9eb0-763c-4b18-ab9d-fcdb1b8f86e5\_1500x649.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":630,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":712,"bytes":200568,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/177793569?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd95f9eb0-763c-4b18-ab9d-fcdb1b8f86e5\_1500x649.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="237.98076923076923" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!vjWG!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd95f9eb0-763c-4b18-ab9d-fcdb1b8f86e5\_1500x649.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d330229-07d8-4c2b-a9e1-ee6a020fb2c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">VISA</a><span>: With inflation on holiday-related items higher this year than last season, nominally reported holiday sales are expected to rise 4.6% year-over-year, up from a 4.3% pace last year.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">After accounting for inflation on holiday items, real spending — a good proxy for foot traffic — is expected to rise just 2.2% this year, down from the 2.5% real spending that occurred last season.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d330229-07d8-4c2b-a9e1-ee6a020fb2c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">2.2% real spending growth</a><span> means fewer items are being purchased, even as dollar volumes increase, suggesting potential challenges for inventory turnover and margin management.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4054e3ff-6830-4d5d-8c0e-7a8438449ffc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":39035,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!0cUN!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/be41a3fd-1525-4a7f-859a-8212e3ab8f68?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Microsoft’s Search and news</a><span> advertising revenue excluding traffic acquisition costs </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4d048303-17ad-4203-ba7c-27d0c47853b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">increased</a><span> 16% (up 15% in constant currency).</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>LinkedIn highlights </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fd0b2e50-30b6-4b0f-bb8c-da9189d88763?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Thought Leader ad opportunities</a><span> in Cam paign Manager.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Microsoft’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/061ed6ce-6f37-479e-998c-3d282de195ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Xbox Console sales fall 29%</a><span> as gaming content and services revenue ticks up.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/793ef539-153a-4f7f-8ae8-18bd71fbdd94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Xbox</a><span> says TikTok is a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/123e765e-f7eb-4d08-bcf9-a90e65b172c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bigger rival</a><span> than PlayStation or Nintendo.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>As part of a strategic shift away from exclusivity, Microsoft is</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ae8a625-86d3-4d97-b5db-56e80280c947?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> bringing Halo to Sony’s competing console</a><span> for the first time.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>HK privacy watchdog reminds LinkedIn users </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9c32ce84-202e-4417-9b41-1b323b078b03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to review</a><span> AI data terms.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Expert panel will </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d7acb4d3-0d51-46c3-940d-e79c6244416f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">determine AGI arrival</a><span> in new Microsoft-OpenAI agreement.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft’s massive AI spending draws </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9fffb6ed-4644-4b25-bc39-c50c0d3ef9e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">investor concerns</a><span> as cloud business booms.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5469098e-32b0-42ad-a257-e9a5642b7492?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bing Search</a><span> adds country/region selector.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>One week to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b87d84e3-0ec8-415d-b155-d6ca466dbd29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">opt out or be fodder</a><span> for LinkedIn AI training.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f38cdf66-4ba9-4f7c-8d16-91be95d2c198?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> 365 Copilot</a><span> now enables you to build apps and workflows.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>LinkedIn reports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7a2d00c1-6532-4dc2-ab4f-9e7e78be6791?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">significant increases</a><span> in post comments and video posts.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9eda6dfb-5213-4dfb-aae6-07a812e24eb3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Q3 revenue</a><span> at LinkedIn rose 9% in constant currency to $4.71 billion.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft Advertising advertiser </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f43dcb3c-6709-4cdb-ba70-5ee2ab3e318d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">console</a><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f43dcb3c-6709-4cdb-ba70-5ee2ab3e318d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">down</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Buck’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c8344d9c-bc56-4eb3-941d-f94251384f14?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new animations for LinkedIn</a><span> bring milestone moments to life.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>StackAdapt </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1dfb4a58-17e9-4199-952a-c58147a913bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">debuts</a><span> map-first DOOH planning.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🥀 Report Break: Experian’s analysis based on over 1.4 billion anonymised transactions across more than 4,000 retail brands reveals that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f26e50a6-4075-4418-ba4d-456994a49cb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Black Friday’s importance as a single day has decreased </a><span>when compared to the broader trading window\* - which increased by more than 10% compared to 2023.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">The value-driven retail (+42% year-on-year), resale marketplace platforms (+21% year-on-year) and social shopping channels (+23.6% year-on-year) are thriving. This growth is particularly strong among the 65+ age group\*\*, who now account for 14% of total online spend, highlighting how people, and especially older demographics, are seeking value, sustainability and convenience.</p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Back on…</p>
